UNIT  Food, Nature and Culture

Learning outcomes

Speaking

- Reorder the steps of an Egyptian food recipe
- Ask and answer questions about the Nile River and the hot desert

Listening

- Identify new vocabulary related to life in the Nile River
- Answer comprehension questions based on a listening text about life in the Nile River

Reading

- Recognize and repeat consonant clusters in common words
- Discuss the moral of a short story
- Identify the sequence of events and the end in a story

Writing

- Complete sentences using the adverbs of frequency
- Write a recipe using sequence words
- Make a flyer about your favorite restaurant

Read the text and answer the questions.

اقرأ النص وأجب عن الأسئلة.

Ahmed and his family went on a trip to Aswan. They went by train. On their way, they enjoyed the Nile River. It's one of the longest rivers in the World. They saw different kinds of colorful fish. Small fish eat plants in the water. These plants are called water weeds. They also saw different kinds of birds. Birds eat small fish, insects and even plants near the river. In Aswan, they saw crocodiles. Crocodiles are the kings of the Nile. Ahmed's father said that they should keep the Nile River clean.

II Listening and Reading

Listen and read. استمع واقرا

Pop Quiz

Animals in the Desert

ندرب على ٣١ من قطع الفهم الحيوانات في الصحراء

The desert is hot and dry, but many animals live there. Snakes often eat small animals like rats or lizards. Camels live in the desert, too. They eat plants to stay strong. Lizards eat insects, which give them energy. Some animals hide under rocks to stay cool during the day. Others, like owls, come out at night when it is cooler. Each animal has an important role in the desert. They help nature stay in balance.

حراامنلا ما ملئرانتيجمال لحاءوه أكللنباكلح اشراتممايمنحها الطاةبعض لحيواناتخئ حتالصخور باردة خلالالنهارالبعض ورممانحراء تساعد الطبيعة على البقاء في توازن.

Language in use

1 The Present Simple Tense

زمن المضارع البسيط : Usage الاستخدام

We use the present simple tense to talk about habits and routines. نستخدم زمن المضارع البسيط للتحدث عن العادات والروتين.

1. Affirmative Statements

االجمل الخبرية المثبتة

| I / You / We / They                          | inf.   |              |
| -------------------------------------------- | ------ | ------------ |
| فاعل جمع                                     | المصدر |              |
| ex: - We usually play tennis on Friday.      |        |              |
| He / She / It                                | inf.   | s / es / ies |
| فاعل مفرد                                    | المصدر |              |
| ex: - Rodina sometimes listens to the radio. |        |              |






لاحظ أن:

يضاف للفعل)مع الفاعل المفرد الغائب

ex:- Marwa usually plays tennis on Fridays.

يضاف للعلمع الفاعل المفرد الغائبإذا كان منتهيا

ex: - My brother often watches TV after lunch.

يضاف للفعل مع الفاعل المفرد الغائبإذا كان منتهيا ب مسبوقة بحرف ساكن ويحذف حرف

ex- Amal always studies hard for her exams.

2. Negative Statements الجمل الخبرية المنفية

| I  | You | We | They | جمع     | don't      | inf. |
| -- | --- | -- | ---- | ------- | ---------- | ---- |
| He | She | It | مد   | doesn't | مصدر الفعل |      |

ex: - I don't play football. - He doesn't get up late.

Adverbs of Frequency ظروف التكرار

| always    | 100% |
| --------- | ---- |
| usually   | 90%  |
| often     | 70%  |
| sometimes | 50%  |
| rarely    | 20%  |
| never     | 0%   |

نستخدم ظروف التكرار للتحدث عن عدد مرات تكرار حدوث شئ ما

ex: - Camels usually eat grass.

تأتي ظروف التكرار قبل الفعل الأساس مثل eat, play, go و بعد الفعل to be مثل am, is, are.

Subject Adverb of frequency Main verb Rest of sentence

Lions never eat grass.

Subject verb to be Adverb of frequency Rest of sentence

Lizards are sometimes dangerous.






Snakes never eat plants. قبل الفعل الأساسي

-- Crocodiles are usually scary. verb to be بعد لاحظ عند استخدام فانفى لا يتم حذف حرمن الفعل إذا كان الفاعل مفرد.

ex :- He never hurts animals.

اقرأ النص واجب عن الأسئلة.

I like different animals, but my favorite animal is the camel. It's a very strong animal. It lives in the desert. It can carry heavy things. The camel can stay for a long time without water. People used camels in the past for carrying things and traveling through the desert. The camel can walk easily in the desert because it has flat feet. I enjoy riding the camel so much.

II Listening and Reading

Listen and read. استمع واقرا

Pop Quiz

The Giving Tree

Once upon a time, there was a boy named Tom who loved a big strong apple tree in his garden. Tom used the tree for everything. He made crowns from its leaves and ate its delicious apples. He rested in its shade and climbed its trunk with his friends. But, Tom never thought about the tree's feelings or needs. He rarely gave it food or water.







ف يوم من الأيام كان هناك ولد يدعى توم وكان يحب شجرة تفاح كبيرة وقوية ف حديفته. استخدم توم الشجرة ف كل شئ. صنع تيجانا من أوراقها وأكل تفاحها اللذيذ كان بستريح ف ظلها ويتسلق جذعها مع اصدقائه ولكن توم لم يفكر ابداً ف مشاعر الشجرة أو احتياجاتها نادرًا ما كان يعطيها طعاما أو ماء.

The Middle

Tom traveled to another place. Years passed and Tom came back home. The tree looked sad. Its leaves bent, and its branches were weak. When Tom saw the tree, he realized that he had been selfish.

سافر توم إلى مكان أخر. ومرت سنوات حتى عاد توم إلى منزله. بدت الشجرة حزينة. أوراقها انحنت واغصانها اصبحت ضعيفة. عندما رأى توم الشجرة أدرك أنه كان أنانيًا.

Tom started to care for the tree, watering it, and protecting it from the sun. Slowly, the tree began to become better. Tom learned that taking care of something meant giving back, and he and the tree became the best of friends.

بدأ توم يعتنى بالشجرة. فسقاهاء ووفر لها الحماية من الشمس. وببطء بدأت الشجرة تتحسن. تعلم توم أن العناية بشئ ما تعني أن نعطي في المقابل وأصبح هو والشجرة أفضل صدقين.

Determine

1. The underlined word strong is the opposite of
- a. weak
- b. sad
- c. tall
- d. huge
2. The underlined phrase care for means
- a. travel away
- b. come back
- c. find out
- d. take care of

: The moral of the story

We should appreciate those who care for us.

يجب أن نقدر أولئك الذين يهتمون بنا.






The story has three main parts

Problem

The boy took more from the tree than he gave.

الولد اخذ من الشجرة اكثر مما أعطاها

Result

Solution

The tree became healthy.

قام الولد بمساعدة الشجرة لتنمو مرة أخرى

The boy learned to care for it like it cared for him.

اصبحت الشجرة بصحة جيدة وتعلم الولد أن يهتم بها كما اهتمت به.

Pronunciation

A consonant cluster is a sequence of two or more consonant sounds without vowels, such as str- in strong or bl- in black.

هو تسلسل من صوتين ساكنين أو أكثر بدون وجود حروف متحركة

They can appear at the beginning, middle, or end of words.

يمكن أن تظهر في بداية أو وسط أو نهاية الكلمات.

Examples: ex -- tree, branches, crocodiles, spring

A consonant cluster of two sounds

truck شاحنة

drink يشرب

plant نبات

محاصيل

II Listening and Reading

Listen and read. .استمع واقرا

Pop Quiz

My favorite Egyptian food

طعامى المصرى المفضل

Koshari is a tasty Egyptian meal with many simple ingredients in one bowl. First, you need to cook rice in a pot with water. In another pot, prepare small pasta. Then, boil lentils in water. For the red sauce, fry some chopped garlic in oil, add tomato sauce, vinegar, and some spices.

الكشرى وجبة مصرية لذيذة. تتكون من مكونات بسيطة في وعاء واحد. أولا: عليك طهى الأرز في إناء مع الماء وف إناء آخر قم بتحضير مكرونة صغيرة. ثم قم بسلق العدس في الماء لتحضير الصلصة الحمراء. قم بقلى الثوم المفروم في الزيت ثم أضف صلصة الطماطم والخل وبعض التوابل.

You also need to make crispy onions. First, cut them into thin slices. Next, fry them in oil until they turn brown and crunchy. After that, put the rice in a bowl and add the pasta and lentils on top. Then, add some chickpeas.

تحتاج أيضا إلى تحضير البصل المقرمش. أولا يفطع إلى شرائح رفيعة. ثم قم بقليه في الزيت حتى يتحول إلى اللون البني ويصبح مقرمشا. بعد ذلك ضع الأرز في إناء واضف المكرونة والعدس فوقه ثم أضف بعض الحمص.

Finally, pour the red sauce all over it, and put the crispy onions on top. There are two more sauces you can add for more flavor. One is a spicy tomato sauce, and the other is Dakka which is made with garlic and vinegar. This mix of foods makes koshari very yummy. Koshari is a traditional Egyptian food.

اخيرزا اسكب الصلصة الحمراء فوقه ثم أضف البصل المقرمش فوقه. هناك نوعان أخران من الصلصات الى يمكن إضافتها للحصول على المزيد من المذاق. إحداهما صلصة طماطم حارة والآخر صلصة الدقة المصنوعة من الثوم والخل. هذا المزيج من المكونات يجعل الكشرى لذيذًا للغاية. الكشرى هو طعام مصرى تقليدى.

Ingredients

| المكونات/مقادير          |                                |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------ |
| 1) عدس                   |                                |
| 2) حمص                   |                                |
| 3) لذيذ                  |                                |
| 4) بصل مقرمش             | 3 large sliced onions          |
| 5) ١ كوب زيت نباتى للقلى | 1 cup vegetable oil for frying |
| 6) صلصة حمراء            | -Red sauce                     |
| 7) صلصة طماطم حارة       | -Spicy tomato sauce            |
| 8) صلصة دقة              | --Dakka sauce                  |






Writing Skill

1 Writing a recipe using sequence words:

كتابة وصفة طهي باستخدام كلمات التسلسل والتتابع:

When writing a recipe, organize the steps using sequence words.

عند كتابة وصفة طعام رتب الخطوات باستخدام كلمات التتابع والتسلسل.

| First,        | أولا    |
| ------------- | ------- |
| Then,         | ثم      |
| After that... | بعد ذلك |
| Next...       | التال   |
| Finally...    | اخيزا   |

Instructions for writing a recipe of your favorite food

تعليمات لكتابة وصفة طهي عن طعامك المفضل

1. Choose your favorite food.
اختر طعامك المفضل.
2. Think about how it's made. What happens first? What comes next?
فكر في كيفية إعداده. ما يحدث أولا وماذا يأتى بعد ذلك.
3. Use sequencing words to organize your steps.
استخدم كلمات التتابع والتسلسل لتنظيم خطواتك.
4. Write 3-4 words showing the steps.
اكتب من (٣ -٤ كلمة لتوضيح هذه الخطوات.
5. Add a title: How to Make Your food name
اضف عنواثاطريقة عمل (اسم طعامك).
6. Glue or draw a picture of your favorite food.
الصق او ارسم صورة لطعامك المفضل.

An example for using sequence words to organize the steps of the Koshari recipe

مثال على استخدام كلمات التسلّسل لتنظيم خطوات عمل وصفة الكشرى

First, cook rice in a pot and pasta in another pot. Then boil lentils.

Next, fry some chopped garlic in oil and add tomato sauce.

Then, make crispy onions, cut them into thin pieces and fry them.

After that, put the rice in a bowl and add the pasta and lentils on top.

Next, pour the red sauce all over it, and put the crispy onions on top.

Finally, add spicy sauce at the end.






Making a flyer about your favorite restaurant

عمل منشور عن مطعمك المفضل

The flyer should have

يجب أن يحتوى المنشور على

| Main Heading | Sub-heading | Image | Menu  | Call to Action |
| ------------ | ----------- | ----- | ----- | -------------- |
| عنوان رئيسى  | عنوان فرعى  | صورة  | قائمة | عبارة تحقيزية  |

1. A main heading is the big title.
. العنوان الرئيسي هو العنوان الكبير.
2. A sub-heading is the smaller title.
٢. العنوان الفرعى هو العنوان الأصغر.
3. An image is a photo or drawing.
٣. الصورة هى صورة فوتوغرافية أو رسمة
4. A menu attracts customers.
٤ قائمة تجذب العملاء.
5. A call to action is an invitation.
الدعوة إلى اتخاذ إجراء ثعد بمثابة دعوة مفتوحة لعميل لاتخاذ إجراء مثللشراء أو التسجيل

Important topics

تعتبر مهارة الكتابة من المهارات الأساسية للتعبيرعن فكرة أو موضوع لذا يجب استخدام جمل امتمقرءه جمل لتساعلتالمعمهارةكجمل

Life in the Nile River

There are many rivers around the world. The Nile River is one of the longest rivers in the world. It is home to many different animals. Crocodiles are strong and dangerous. They hunt large animals in or near the water. Small fish in the river eat water weeds. Birds eat insects, small fish, and plants near the river.

Animals in the desert

The desert is hot and dry, but many animals live there such as camels, snakes, and foxes. Snakes often eat small animals like rats or lizards. Camels live in the desert, too. They eat plants to stay strong. Lizards eat insects, which give them energy. Each animal has an important role in the desert.

The importance of giving back

Giving is important. When we give to others, we show care. We should appreciate those who care for us. We shouldn't be selfish and care for things which help us.

My favorite Egyptian food

My favorite Egyptian food is Koshari. Koshari is very yummy. Koshari is a tasty Egyptian meal with simple ingredients in one bowl. People pour the red sauce all over it, and put the crispy onions on top.

UNIT My Healthy Body
2

Learning Outcomes

Speaking

- Ask and answer questions about healthy habits
- Talk about personal healthy habits
- Recognize and repeat one-syllable words

Reading

- Answer questions related to a text on healthy habits
- Discuss healthy habits based on the reading text

Listening

- Identify and understand vocabulary related to health
- Listen to a dialog and answer comprehension questions

Writing

- Distinguish between countable and uncountable nouns, and apply the correct determiners in context
- Write sentences on how to stay healthy
- Write a paragraph about a healthcare giver

Listening and Reading

How do you take care of your health?

Mom, why is it important to play sports?

Well, sports help us stay healthy and make our bodies stronger.

Oh! So if I play football, will I be stronger?

Yes, it makes your heart strong and helps your muscles grow. It also increases energy.

What about swimming?

Swimming is very good, too! It makes your lungs strong. In fact, it helps your whole body.






Good, but sometimes I get tired when I play sports.

Mazen حستالك أعرعأحياتاندما أمارسلرياضة

That's normal. Try eating some fruit before you exercise to give you more energy.

Alright. Sports make us fit too, right?

بالضبطا التمرين يساعد على إنقاص الوزن وتقوية العظام Mom

Absolutely! Exercising regularly helps you sleep better and reduces stress.

II Listening and Reading

Lok adreadو

Adam's journey to a healthier life

رحلة آدم على نحو حياة أكثر صحة

Hello! I'm Adam and I'd like to share how my habits have changed. In the past, I woke up late on weekends and didn't exercise much.

مرحتا اسمى أدم و أود أن أشارك كيف عاداتي تغيرت. ف الماضي كنت أستيقظ متأخرزا ف عطلات نهاية الأسبوع ولم أكن أمارس الرياضة كثيرا.

1) عادات

ا

57






Now, I wake up early and exercise every morning -- it makes me feel strong and happy. I used to watch TV all day, but now I play football with friends every Saturday.

قبل، استيقظ باكرًا وأتمرن كل صباح لأنها تشعرني بالقوة والسعادة. كنت معتادًا على مشاهدة التلفاز طوال اليوم ولكن الآن ألعب كرة القدم مع الأصدقاء كل سبت.

Before, I loved chips, burgers, and sweets. Now, I eat more fruits and vegetables, which give me important vitamins. My favorite snacks are apples, bananas, and carrots. And I drink water instead of soda.

من قبل أحببت البطاطس المقلية والبرجر والحلويات. الآن أناول المزيد من الفاكهة والخضروات حبث تعطينى فيتامينات مهمة. وجباتى الخفيفة المفضلة هى التفاح والموز والجزر. وأشرب الماء بدلا من المياه الغازية.

My family's habits changed too - we grill or bake food instead of frying like we used to do in the past, and we eat dinner early followed by a walk.

تغيرت عادات عائلتي أيضا. الآن نشوي أو نخبز الطعام بدلا من أن نقليه كما اعتدنا أن نفعل من قبل، وفي الماضي نتناول العشاء باكرًا متبوعًا بالمشي.

These changes give me more energy and help me feel better. Have you changed your habits too?

هذه التغييرات تعطينا طاقة أكثر وتساعدنا على الشعور بالتحسن. هل غيرت عاداتك أيضا؟

Language in use

Countable nouns

Uncountable nouns

| الاسم المرد الذييعد يسبق بأداه نكرة | الاسم الذيا يعدلا يسبق بأداة نكرة |
| ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------- |

| oranges                             | milk                              |

an:

We use a before a singular countable noun -- starting with a consonant sound.

تستخدم قبل الاسم المفرد المعدود الذي يبدأ بصوت ساكن

a banana

a habit

ex: - I eat a banana every day.

an

We use an before a singular countable noun starting with a vowel sound.

تستخدم قبل الاسم المفرد المعدود الذى يبدا بصوت متحرك.

an egg

an umbrella

ex: - She eats an egg in the morning.

Usage

الاستخدام

some &#x26; any

We use some &#x26; any with plural countable nouns and uncountable nouns.

تستخدم &#x26;مع الأسماء الجمع المعدودة والأسماء غير المعدودة

some بعض

in affirmative sentences

تأي ف الجمل الخبرية المثبتة

ex: - She drank some water. (جملة خبرية مثبته)





in negative sentences

any أى  تأتي في الجمل الخبرية المنفية

in questions

تأتى في صيفة السؤال

: ex- There aren't any apples left              )صيغة النفى

--Do you have any water ?                       )صيغة السؤال

There are many ways to stay healthy. One of these ways is to eat healthy food like fruits and vegetables. Fruits and vegetables give us vitamins. Playing sports is another way. We should play sports every day to be fit and strong.

Read the text and answer the questions.

I'm Adam, and I'd like to share my habits how have changed. In the past, I woke up late on weekends and didn't exercise much. Now, I wake up early and exercise every morning - it makes me feel strong and happy. I used to watch TV all day, but now I play football with friends every Saturday. Before, I loved chips, burgers, and sweets. Now, I eat more fruits and vegetables, which give me important vitamins. My favorite snacks are apples, bananas, and carrots. And I drink water instead of soda. My family's habits changed too - we grill or bake food instead of frying like we used to do in the past, and we eat dinner early followed by a walk.

Listening and Reading
Our Body's Super Pump!

Pop Quiz

We have an amazing body. The heart is one of the most important organs of our body. It is about the size of your fist. The heart's job is to pump blood to all parts of the body.

لدينا جسم مدهش. القلب من أهم أعضاء الجسم. إنه بحجم قبضة اليد. وظيفة القلب ضخ الدم إلي جميع أنحاء الجسم.

Blood carries oxygen and nutrients to every cell. It also takes away waste like carbon dioxide. The heart pumps blood through blood vessels, which are like small tubes.

يحمل الدم الأكسجين والعناصر الغذائية إلى كل خلية. كما ينخلص من الفضلات كأكسيد الكربون. القلب يضخ الدم عبر الأوعية الدموية، التي تشبه الأنابيب الصغيرة.

The heart is a strong muscle that keeps us alive. Too much stress can hurt your heart, so it's important to keep it healthy. You can take care of your heart by eating good food, sleeping well, and drinking a lot of water. Also, exercise helps your heart pump better and keeps you from getting sick. If you follow these tips, your heart will stay strong. It may be small, but it is very powerful!

القلب عضلة قوية تبقينا على قيد الحياة. قد يؤذي التوتر الشديد قلبك لذا من المهم جداً الحفاظ على صحته. يمكنك العناية بقلبك بتناول طعام جيد، النوم جيداً، وشرب الكثير من المياه. كما أن ممارسة الرياضة تساعد قلبك على الضخ بشكل أفضل وتساعد على الوقاية من الأمراض. إذا اتبعت هذه النصائح يمكنك الحفاظ على قوة قلبك. قلبك قد يكون صغير ولكنه قوي!

A syllable is a word part with one vowel sound.

المقطع الصوتى هو جزء من الكلمة يحتوى على صوت متحرك واحد.

One-syllable words

كلمات ذات مقطع واحد:

| arm   | ذراع      |
| ----- | --------- |
| jump  | يقفر      |
| pot   | إناء      |
| bread | خبز       |
| leaf  | ورقة شجرة |
| fd    | يطعم      |
| rn    | يجرى      |
| flag  | علم       |
| tr    | شجرة      |
| fish  | سمكة      |
| hat   | قبعة      |

Notes

--A syllable can have one or more vowel letters but only one vowel sound.

- يمكن أن يحتوى المقطع الصوتى على حرف متحرك أو أكثر ولكن يحتوى على صوت متحرك واحد فقط.

- Words like bread (5 letters) and pot (3 letters) look different but both have one syllable because they only have one vowel sound.

هناك كلمات مثل bread تكون من خمسة أحرف و pot تكون من ثلاثة أحرف تبدو مختلفة لكن كلاهما يحتوى على مقطع صوتى واحد فقط لأنها تحتوى على صوت متحرك واحد.

Listening and Reading

Looreaaarظراقرأ وتعلم

Healthcare Heroes

الأبطال العاملين في مجال الرعاية الصحية

Healthcare workers - like doctors, nurses, pharmacists, and paramedics are real-life heroes who spend their lives helping others. They work day and night to keep people healthy and treat illnesses. Doctors use their knowledge and skills to check patients, diagnose diseases, and provide treatments. Nurses often work around the clock to give support and care for patients.

العاملون في مجال الرعاية الصحية كالأطباء والممرضين والصيادلة والمسعفين هم أبطال حقيقيون يكرسون حياتهم لمساعدة الآخرين. يعملون ليلاً ونهاراً للحفاظ على صحة الناس وعلاج الأمراض. يستخدم الأطباء معارفهم ومهاراتهم لفحص المرضى وتشخيص الأمراض وتقديم العلاج. غالباً ما تعمل الممرضات على مدار الساعة لتقديم الدعم والرعاية للمرضى.






Healthcare Workers: Our Everyday Heroes

Pharmacists play a very important role, too. They make sure patients receive the correct medicine and give advice on how to use it safely. Paramedics respond quickly in emergencies. They give help on the spot and take patients safely to hospitals. Healthcare workers also help us live healthier lives as they encourage us to eat healthy meals, stay active, play sports and stop harmful habits.

يلعب أبضا الصيادلة دورا بالغ الأهمية. فهم بتأكدون من حصول المرضي على الدواء المناسب ويقدمون لهم النصائح حول الاستخدام الآمن للأدوية. وبستجيب المسعفون بسرعة ف حالات الطوارئ وبقدمون مساعدة كبيرة على الفور ويتقلون المرض بأمان إلى المستشفيات. كما بساعدنا العاملون ف مجال الرعاية الصحية على عبش حياه أكثر صحة لأنه بشجعنا على تناول وجبات صحية. والنشاط البدني وممارسة الرياضة. والتوقف عن العادات الضارة.

These heroes work long hours with kindness. We should thank them by caring for our health and showing them respect. Healthcare workers are really our everyday heroes!

يعمل هؤلاء الأبطال لساعات طويلة بكل لطف. علينا أن نشكرهم بالاهتمام بصحتنا واحترامهم. فالعاملون في مجال الرعاية الصحية هم أبطالنا الحقيقيون.

| اغسل بدك قبل وبعد الأكل | تناول الفاكهة والخضروات بانتظام     | اشرب الكافي من المياه يوميا            |
| Exercise regularly      | Sleep early and get enough rest     | Brush your teeth daily with toothpaste |
| نمرن بانتظام            | نام مبكزا وخذ قسط كاف من الراحة     | اغسل أسنانك بوميا بمعجون أسنان         |






Writing Skill

Writing a non-fiction text about a healthcare person

هل المعلومات المستخدمة صحيحة

When you write a non-fiction text, ask yourself:

هل قمت بتأليف شيتا ما

عند كتابة نص واقعي اسأل نفسك:

هل المصدر الخاص بي موثوق

Non-fiction writing should include

يجب أن تشتمل الكتابة الواقعية على

| An introductory sentence                                         | A closing sentence                                 |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| جملة مقدمة                                                       | جملة خاتمة                                         |
| My neighbor, Selim, is a doctor at our local hospital.           | The work is tiring, but he's proud to be a doctor. |
| Details that are all true                                        | Adjective                                          |
| تفاصيل صحيحة                                                     | صفات                                               |
| He wears a white coat and carries a stethoscope around his neck. | white, big, kind                                   |

Creating a poster

The elements of a good poster

1. A clear title --- to grab the attention.
2. Organized steps to explain.
3. Photos or drawings --- to illustrate.

- عنوانا واضخا لجذب الانتباه
- خطوات منظمة للتوضيح
- الصور أو رسومات للتوضيح

Read the text and answer the questions.

Healthcare workers like doctors, nurses, pharmacists, and paramedics are real-life heroes who spend their lives helping others. They work day and night to keep people healthy and treat illnesses. Doctors use their knowledge and skills to check patients, diagnose diseases and provide treatments. Pharmacists play a very important role, too. They make sure patients receive the correct medicine and give advice on how to use it safely. Paramedics respond quickly in emergencies. They give help on the spot and take patients to hospitals.

Importanا topics

تعتبر مهارة الكتابة من المهارات الأساسية للتعبير عن فكرة أو موضوع لذا يجب استخدام جمل وأفكار مترابطة. قم بقراءة هذه الجمل لتساعدك على التعامل مع مهارة الكتابة.

1. Sports

Sports are very important. Sports help us stay healthy and make our bodies stronger. Football makes our heart strong and helps our muscles grow. Swimming is very good, too. It makes our lungs strong. Exercising regularly helps you sleep better and reduces stress.

2. Healthy habits

Healthy habits are important for our bodies and minds. They help us feel good and do well. We should eat a lot of fruits and vegetables. Fruits and vegetables give us important vitamins. We should also drink a lot of water.

3. The heart is a super pump

The heart is one of the most important organs of our body. It is about the size of your fist. The heart's job is to pump blood to all parts of the body. The heart is a strong muscle that keeps us alive.

4. Healthcare workers

Healthcare workers like doctors and nurses are real-life heroes. They work day and night to keep people healthy and treat illnesses. Doctors use their knowledge to check patients and provide treatments. Nurses often work around the clock to give support and care for patients.

Dina, did you hear about the flood in our city last week? It was terrible. دينا هل سمعت عن الفيضان في مدينتنا الأسبوع الماضى؟ لفد كان سيتا للغاية.






Yes, Mazen! I saw it on the news. The water covered the streets, and many cars got stuck. It looked scary.

نعم, با مازن لقد رأيتها ف الأخبار المياه غطت الشوارع  علقت العديد من السيارات. كان الأمر مخيفا

Yes, my uncle's house was damaged. The water flooded his living room, and he had to stay with us for a few days.

نعم لقد ضرر منزل عمي وغمرت المياه غرفة معيشته واضطر للبقاء معنا لبضعة أبام.

That's so sad. I hope he's okau now. Floods are terrible, And now there's a drought -- no rain in China for months! Farmers can't grow crops because there's no water.

ذلك شيء محزن أمل أن يكون بخير الآن الفيضانات تكون سيئة جلّا  -والآن هناك جفافا - لا بوجد أمطار لشهور ف الصين لا يستطبع المزارعون زراعة المحاصيل بسبب عدم وجود مباه

نعم الفيضانات فظيعة! والأرض تجف جذا لدرجة أن الحيوانات لا تجد المياه وسمعت عن حريق بنتشر في الغابة في الولابات المتحدة الأمربكية

Oh no! Is it close to the mountains?

أوه لا١ هل هو بالقرب من الجبال

Yes, and it spreads quickly because it's dry there and the winds are so strong. Sadly many trees burned down, and animals lost their homes.

نعم وتنتشر النيران بسرعة بسبب الجفاف الشديد هناك والرياح فوية جذا للأسف احترقت العديد من الأشجار وفقدت الحبوانات منازلها

What a pity! Let's learn more about how we can stay safe and help our planet.

ويا للأسف. دعنا نتعلم المزيد عن كيفية البقاء آمنين و مساعدة كوكبنا.

Read the text and answer the questions.

Some places in the world have different types of extreme weather. You can find floods which cause great damage. The water covers streets and many cars are stuck. It's scary. Also water can reach and come into homes. A Wildfire is another type of extreme weather which happens in the forests of some places. Fires can spread quickly when it's so dry. Many trees can be burned and animals can lose their homes. Drought also is a dangerous type of extreme weather. It happens when there is no rain for a long time. We can't grow many crops or keep animals.

تيe Weather in Ancient Egupt

الطقس في مصر القديمة

Ancient Egupt had weather that made farming possible, even in the desert. The Nile River gave water and rich soil, which helped crops grow. But sometimes, the weather also brought problems like floods, droughts, and sandstorms.

تمتعت مصر القديمة بطقس مكنها من الزراعة حتى في الصحراء. لقد وفر نهر النيل الماء والتربة الخصبة مما ساعد على نمو المحاصيل. ولكن في بعض الأحيان كانت هناك مشاكل كبيرة كالفيضانات والجفاف والعواصف الرملية.

Floods

The Nile River flooded every year. The water brought rich soil to the land, which helped crops grow. But sometimes, the floods were too strong and damaged farms. To stop this, the Ancient Eguptians built canals and basins to control the water and spread it over their fields.

كان نهر النيل يفيض سنوياً. جالباً معه الماء والتربة الخصبة إلى الأرض مما ساعد على نمو المحاصيل. لكن في بعض الأحيان كانت الفيضانات تسبب أضراراً للمزارع. ولإيقاف هذا، بنى المصريون القدماء قنوات وأحواضاً للتحكم في المياه وتوزيعها على حقولهم.

Droughts

Droughts happened when there was not enough rain. The Nile's water became low, and the land became dry. Crops could not grow. To solve this, the Ancient Eguptians made irrigation systems to bring water to their fields.

كان الجفاف يحدث عندما تقل الأمطار مما أدى إلى انخفاض منسوب مياه النيل وجفاف الأرض. لم تنمو المحاصيل. تعلم المصريون القدماء استخدام أنظمة الري لجلب المياه إلى حقولهم.

Sandstorm

Sandstorms, called Khamsin, brought strong, hot winds with sand. It was hard to see and breathe. People stayed inside to stay safe. They built their homes with thick mud bricks to protect themselves from the wind and sand.

كانت العواصف الرملية، تسمى الخماسين، كانت هذه الرياح شديدة الحرارة وقوية تحمل الرمال. مما جعل الرؤية والتنفس صعبين. لزم الناس منازلهم خلال هذه العواصف حفاظاً على سلامتهم. بنوا منازلهم بطوب طين سميك لحمايتهم من الرياح والرمال.






The Ancient Egyptians

The Ancient Egyptians didn't have modern machines, but they found smart ways to survive. They worked hard, used simple tools, and protected themselves from heat, sand, and floods.

لللمرييماءديهدوفلاءععلوست أدوات بسيطة. وقاموا بحماية أنفسهم من الحرارة والرمال والفيضانات.

II Listening and Reading

Lookaread.اظوا

wo Frog  الضفدعان

The beginning

Once upon a time, in a small and quiet village, there were two frogs who were best friends. They lived together in a small pond near the edge of the village. The pond was their home, and they loved it very much. Every day, they had fun catching flies, swimming in the cool water, and resting under the warm sun.

فديم لزمانف فري صغيرة هادةكان هناك دعان صديان مقريين عاشا معا فبركة صغيرة رب أطر رية

The middle

One summer, the weather became extremely hot. The sun was strong, and the pond started to dry up. The frogs got worried. They knew they couldn't stay in the pond any longer because there wasn't enough water for them. They decided to leave the pond and look for a new home where they could live safely.

حللأاجدأأ لدعانأنهم يساات لمجممنماء ماراركمجديد يعيشان فيه بأمان.

The end

The two frogs leaped through fields and grass, looking for another pond. After a long time, they saw a deep well. One of the frogs said, "This well looks like a great place for us to live! The water is cool, and it's very deep. We can swim here all day."

با مكانا عً لن ماء هدأو

The other frog thought for a moment and said, "The water is nice now but what if it dries up one day? If that happens, we will get stuck at the bottom of the well, and we won't be able to get out."

ع نتمكن من الخروج

The first frog thought about what his friend said, then agreed. They decided not to live in the well. Instead, they kept leaping and searching for a safe home.





The moral of the story

الدرس المستفاد من القصة:

Its important to think before we make decisions علينا التفكير قبل اتخاذ القرارات

Read the text and answer the questions.

Last week, I read an interesting story. It was about two frogs. The two frogs were best friends. They lived together in a small pond near the edge of the village. One summer, the sun was strong, and the pond started to dry up. The frogs got worried. They decided to leave the pond. The two frogs leaped through fields and grass, looking for another pond. After a long time, they saw a deep well. One of the frogs thought it was a great place for them to live! The water was cool, and it was very deep. The other frog thought it wasn't safe for them. They decided not to live in the well. Instead, they kept leaping and searching for a safe home.

II Listening and Reading

Pop Quiz

تدرب على ٣١ من قطع الفهم ونصوص الاستماع

The beginning

Once upon a time, in a cozy little house, lived a girl named Lily. One sunny morning, Lily looked out her window and saw something strange. Big, fluffy clouds were floating upside down! Oh no! she cried. How will my flowers get sunshine if the clouds are upside down?

ذات مرة في بيت صغير دافئ كانت تعيش فتاة اسمها ليلى. في صباح مشمس نظرت ليلى من نافذتها ورأت شيئًا غريبًا. كانت غيوم كبيرة ومنتفخة تطفو في السماء مقلوبة رأسًا على عقب. أوه لا! صاحت: كيف ستحصل زهورى على أشعة الشمس؟

The middle

Lily thought hard. She remembered her grandma saying that sometimes a gentle whisper could fix things. So, Lily opened her window and whispered, "Please, dear clouds, turn right side up!" Slowly and magically, the clouds began to flip.

فكرت ليلى بعمق. تذكرت جدتها وهى تقول إن لهمسة اللطيفة أحيانًا يمكن أن تصلح الأمور. لذا فتحت ليلى نافذتها وهمست بلطف: "من فضلك أيتها الغيوم العزيزة، ارجعي للجهة الصحيحة!" وببطء وسحر بدأت الغيوم في الدوران.

One by one, they turned over, showing their bright, sunny sides. Lily clapped her hands with joy. The sun shone brightly, and the flowers smiled. Lily knew that even big problems could be solved with a little bit of kindness and a gentle whisper.

انقلبوا واحدًا تلو الآخر ليكشفوا عن وجوههم المشرقة والمليئة بالبهجة. صفقت ليلى بيديها من الفرح. أشرفت الشمس ساطعة وابتسمت الأزهار. عرفت ليلى أن حتى أكبر المشاكل يمكن حلها بقليل من اللطف وهمسة رقيقة.

2 Important topics

تعتبر مهارة الكتابة من المهارات الأساسية للتعبير عن فكرة أو موضوع لذا يجب استخدام جمل وأفكار مترابطة. قم بقراءة هذه الجمل لتساعدك على التعامل مع مهارة الكتابة )٨ جملة

Floods in Ancient Egypt

The Nile River flooded every year in Ancient Egypt. The water brought rich soil to the land which helped crops grow. Sometimes the floods were too strong and damaged farms. The Ancient Egyptians built canals and basins to control the water. These canals helped to spread water over their fields.

2 Extreme weather

Droughts, wildfires, and heat waves are extreme weather events. Droughts happen when there is no rain for a long time. The land gets dry. Animals can find water. Wildfires are dangerous. Wildfires burn through forests and homes. Wildfires spread quickly when it's dry. Animals lose their homes because of wildfires.

The weather in Ancient Egypt

Ancient Egypt had weather that made farming possible. The weather in Ancient Egypt brought some problems like floods, droughts, and sandstorms. Sandstorms brought strong, hot winds with sand. It was hard to see and breathe. People stayed inside during these storms to stay safe. They built their homes with thick mud bricks to protect themselves from the wind and sand.

Learning outcomes

Listening

- Identify specific places by listening to descriptive passages

Speaking

- Ask and answer questions about favorite places

- ت على لمفردات الل المع بالأماكفالمجتمع
- Identify trigraph sounds in spoken words
- يحدد أصوات الحروف الثلائية فى الكلمات المنظوفة

Writing

- Write a first person narrative describing a place or experience using clear structure, personal voice, and descriptive language
- ك سرذا بضمير المتكلم يصف مكانا أو تجرية باستخدام بنية واضحة وأسلوب شخصى ولغة وصفية
- Use prepositions of time in context
- بستخدم حروف الجر الخاصة بالوقت فى سياق

Reading

- Demonstrate understanding of texts about village life and new city developments
- بوضح فهم نصوص عن حياة الفرية و تطورات المدينة الجديدة
- Demonstrate understanding of word problems
- بظهر فهم المسائل اللفظية

II Listening and Reading

Look rea an lear. انظر اقرأ وتعلم

Life in my village

الحياة في قريى

I live in a small village with green fields, tall trees, and quiet hills. The people are friendly, and the village is full of life.

My family and I live in a cozy cottage near the Community center. Every morning, I hear birds singing and see farmers working in the fields. The air is fresh, and everything feels calm.

أعيش في قرية صغيرة بها الحقول الخضراء والأشجار العالية والتلال الهادئة. أهل القرية طيبون وهى مليئة بالحياة.

أنا وعائلتي نعيش في كوخ مريح بالقرب من المركز المجتمعي. صباحاً أسمع رفة الطيور وأرى المزارعين يعملون في الحقول. الهواء نقي وكل شئ يتسم بالهدوء.

The main square is the busiest place. On Saturdays, people meet there to shop at the market. Farmers keep animals like cows, sheep, and chickens in the barns. I visit my uncle's barn to help feed the animals.

الميدان الرئيسي هو أكثر الأماكن ازدحاماً. أيام السبت يجتمع الناس هناك للتسوق في السوق. يربي المزارعون الحيوانات مثل الأبقار والأغنام والدجاج في الحظائر. أزور حظيرة عمي لأساعده في إطعام الحيوانات.

The community center is a fun place. I meet my friends and play games there every Thursday.

المركز المجتمعي مكان ممتع. أقابل أصدقائي وألعب الألعاب هناك كل خميس.

At night, the village is quiet. I walk near the fields and look at the stars. I love my village. It is a happy place to live in.

الليل تكون القرية هادئة. أستطيع قرب الحقول وانظر إلى النجوم. أحب قريتي فهي مكان سعيد للعيش.

III Language in use

repositions of time

We use prepositions of time at, on and in, to say when something happens.

نستخدم حروف الجر الخاصة بالوقت at، on و in لنتحدث عن وقت حدوث شىء ما

We use at with:

- clock times - at 3 p.m. - الوفت (الساعة)
- at 9 o'clock
- ex: - I get up at 8 o'clock every day.
- Time phrases - at lunchtime
- - at breakfast - عبارات زمنية
- - at midnight - في منتصف الليل
- - at night
- - at noon - في وقت الظهيرة

We use on with:

- Days - on Saturdays - أيام الأسبوع
- - on Friday
- ex: The party is on Tuesday - التواريخ
- on Dates - on April 26th
- - on March 8th
- ex: - My birthday is on June 29th - عبارات زمنية
- - on holiday - في إجازة
- - on time - في الوقت المحدد
- - on a sunny day - في يوم مشمس
- - on school days - في أيام الدراسة

We use in with:

- seasons, months and years - فصول السنة والشهور والسنوات
- - in spring
- - in summer
- - in winter
- - in autumn - فصول السنة
- - in January
- - in March
- - in September
- - in 2012
- - in 1989
- - in 2000
- ex: - In summer, I go to the beach.
- Parts of the day - فترات اليوم
- - in the morning
- - in the afternoon
- - in the evening
- ex: - I like visiting my uncle in the afternoons.

Lesson 3 Multiply it with words!

Pronunciation

Read and learn. اقرأ وتعلم

ألتضرب عدد مكون من رقمين في عدد مكون من رقم واحد:

A farmer has  boxes of apples. If each box has 32 apples, then how many apples does the farmer have in total?

1. First, we need to understand the question.
2. - How many apples?
- How many boxes?

Then, we put the double-digit on the top, and the single-digit on the bottom.
3. Finally, we multiply!

Here, we need to multiply the double-digit number by the single-digit number:

هنا تحتاج إلى ضرب عدد مكون من رقمين في عدد مكون من رقم واحد:

| tens | ones |
| ---- | ---- |
| 3    | 4    |

So, 3 tens 4 ones 12 tens

أى ٣ عشرات مضرونا في ٤ أحاد -٢١ عشرات

Problem 32?

Solution 128

Answer 2 apples

Pronunciation

A trigraph is a group of three letters that represents a single sound.

هي مجموعة من ثلاثة أحرف تتمثل في صوتاً واحداً.

| The trigraph tch      | The trigraph dge      | The trigraph igh      |
| --------------------- | --------------------- | --------------------- |
| sounds like ch        | sounds like i         | sounds like           |
| The trigraph tch make | The trigraph dge make | The trigraph igh make |
| the sound /tf         | the sound /dz!        | the sound /aI         |
| match                 | مباراة                | bridge                |
| كوبرى                 | ight                  | صحيح                  |
| light                 | رحلة جوبة             | watch                 |
| ساعة يد               | fridge                | ثلاجة                 |
| Witch                 | ساحرة                 | bright                |
| لامع                  |                       |                       |

Notice

Example: bridge is pronounced bri

match is pronounced mach

right is pronounced rite

Look lst rea.اأنظر اتمع وارأ وتصوص الاستماع

Egypt's New City

Egypt builds new cities almost everywhere. My family and I live in the New Administrative Capital, 15 km east of Cairo. It's a new city that helps reduce crowding in Cairo. The city is designed to be very smart. Modern technology is used to manage roads, water, energy and almost everything. The city has green areas everywhere. It has also government offices and important buildings. The city where I live has the tallest building in Africa, the Iconic Tower, and a new airport! The Iconic Tower is 385 meters high and has 80 floors. It is made of strong glass and steel. From the top, I can see the whole city! People here can find great jobs and have a peaceful life. It is my favorite city in Egypt.

الراة 144




تبى مصر مدتا جديدة تقربتا فيكل مكان. انتقلت عائلى وأنا إلى العاصمة الإدارية الجديدة الى تقع على بعد 45كيلومتزا شرق القاهرة. إنها مدينة جذابة تساعد على تقليلالازدحام فالفاهرةتم تصميم المدينة لتكون ذكية جذا. حيث نستخدم التكنولوجيا لإدارة المياه والطاقة ومعظم الأشياء الأخرى. المدينة مليئة بالمناطق الخضراء ف كل مكانوتضم مكاتب حكومية ومباني مهمة أخرى المدينة الى أعيش فيها بها أطول مبى ف أفريقيا وهو البرج الأيقونيويوجد مطار جديد أيضا بيلغ ارتفاع البرج الأيقونى 38متزاويتكون من 80طابقاوهو مصنوع من الزجاج والفولاذ القويمن أعلى البرج يمكني رؤية المدينة بأكملهاالناس هنا يمكنهم العثور على وظائف رائعة ويعيشون حياة هادئةإنها مدينى المفصلة ف مصر.

III Writing skill

: VWriting a Narrative Text in the First Person

كتابة نص سردى بصيغة المتكلم

1. First Person : Use I, me, my
المتكلم استخدم عندما تكت فصة بصيغة المتكلم كأنك تحكى فصة عن نفسك
2. Setting : Where is your story happening ?
المكان :أين تحدث قصتك ٢ يجب أن تذكر المكان الذى تدور فيه أحداث القصة مثلا في القرية
3. Goal Setting : What do you want to do?
تحديد الهدف:ماذا تريد أن تفعل ٢ وضح ما هو هدفك في القصة.
4. Description : Use the 5 senses and other adjectives.
الوصف :استخدم كلمات الحواس الخمس والصفات الأخرىي. ماذا رأيت سمعت مع الصفات لتوضيح المشهد مثل كبير.
5. Ending : How does the story finish ?
النهاية :كيف تنتهي القصة ٢ يجب أن تضع نهاية واضحة للقصة

The word narrative comes from narration or telling a story.

كلمةaatvتعىسرد أو رواية قصة

Don't forget that a good story is one that readers can see in their minds limagineJ.

لا تنش أن القصة الجيدة هى تلك الى تجعل القارئ يتخيل الأحداث ف عقله.

Good writers use a lot of description to do this.

الكتاب الجيدون يستخدمون الكثير من الوصف لجعل القصة حيّة ومشوقة

المعاصر لفة إغبليزية /٥ب/رم١) 145





Research

Why do we research anything? We research to learn something new-something we didn't know before.

Steps

1. Pick a topic. اختر موضوغًا.
2. Ask a question? اسأل سؤال
3. Look for information. ابحث عن المعلومات.
4. Organize what you find. نظّم ما وجدته.

Example

Tramids الأهرامات

When were they built? قى تم بنانها

-- Built between 2600 and 2500 BC, in Giza. Khofu, Khafre, Menkaure. ت ما بين ٥٢و ٥٢قبل الميلاد فى الجيزة. خوفو خفرع ومنقاورع

List the information in complete sentences. تب المعلومات في جمل كاملة.

Step 1: Read and learn how to answer

Step 2: Read and answer the questions

I'm Mazen. I live in a village surrounded by green fields, trees, and hills. The village is small, but it was full of life and friendly people. My family and I live in a cozy cottage in the middle of the village. Every morning, I hear the birds singing and see farmers working in the fields. The air is fresh and everything feels calm.

I'm Marwan. I live in the New Administrative Capital, 45 km east of Cairo. It's a new city that helps reduce crowding in Cairo. The city is designed to be very smart. Modern technology is used to manage roads, water, energy, and almost everything. The city has green areas everywhere. It has also government offices and important buildings.

2 Important topics

تعتبر مهارة الكتابة من المهارات الأساسية للتعبيرعن فكرة أو موضوع لذا يجب استخدام جمل وأفكار مترابطة. قم بقراءة هذه الجمل لتساعدك على التعامل مع مهارة الكتابة.

Places in our community

There are many places in our community for food, drink or fun. We can relax and drink coffee at a coffee shop. We can buy groceries at a supermarket. We can have dinner at a restaurant. We can go on rides at a theme park. We can buy bread at a bakery. We can go to the club to play sports.

A new city in Egypt

The New Administrative Capital in Egypt is a new city in Cairo. It helps reduce crowding in Cairo. It has government offices and important buildings. The city has green areas everywhere. People can find great jobs there.

3 Life in a village

Life in a village is peaceful and simple. People live in cozy cottages surrounded by green fields and fresh air. There is a market in the main square where people buy and sell fresh fruit. Farmers keep their animals in barns. Children have fun at the community center.

Language in use

Prepositions of time:

We use at with clock times, mealtimes and some time phrases.

ex.: I get up at 6 o'clock every day.

We use in with parts of day, months, and seasons.

ex.: The weather is hot in summer.

We use on with days of the week and some time phrases.

ex.: My birthday is on January 5th.

ونصوص الاستماع

Mr. Hani and his students are discussing natural resources.

يتحدث الأستاذ هانى وطلابه عن الموارد الطبيعية في مصر.

Good morning class! Today we will talk about natural resources. Do you know what natural resources are?

صباح الخير با طلابي! سنتحدت اليوم عن الموارد الطبيعية. هل تعرفون ما هى الموارد الطبيعية؟

Yes! They are things we find in nature, like water and trees.

نعم ١ هى الأشياء الى نجدها في الطبيعة مثل المياه والأشجار. Ali




Natural Resources in Egypt

Very good! Natural resources help us in our daily lives. Can you name a natural resource we can find in Egypt?

Mr. Hani: رائع! الموارد الطبيعية تساعدنا في حياتنا اليومية. هل يمكنك أن تذكر مورداً طبيعياً يمكننا إيجاده في مصر؟

The Nile River! It provides water for drinking and farming.

نهر النيل! فهو يمدنا بالمياه للشرب والزراعة. Mona

Excellent! The Nile is the most important water resource in Egypt. What other treasures does Egypt have?

Mr. Hani: رائع! نهر النيل هو أهم مصدر للمياه في مصر. ماذا عن الكنوز الأخرى لدى مصر؟

We have oil and natural gas. My uncle works for an oil company.

لدينا النفط والغاز الطبيعي. يعمل عمي في شركة نفط. Ali

That's right! Oil and natural gas are used to make fuel and electricity. What about farming?

Mr. Hani: هذا صحيح! النفط والغاز الطبيعي يتم استخدامهم في صناعة الوقود وتوليد الكهرباء. وماذا عن الزراعة؟

We grow cotton, wheat, and many kinds of fruit.

نحن نزرع القطن والقمح وأنواع كثيرة من الفاكهة. Mona

Well done! The fertile land along the Nile and in the Delta region helps us grow food.

Mr. Hani: أحسنت! الأرض الخصبة على طول نهر النيل وفي منطقة الدلتا تساعدنا على زراعة الطعام.

I read that Egypt has minerals, too, like gold!

لقد قرأت أن مصر بها معادن أيضاً مثل الذهب. Ali

المعاصر لفة إقليزية//قرم 161




That's right!

Egupt's deserts contain gold, iron, and other minerals.

هذا صحيح ١ تحتوى صحارى مصر على ذهب حديد ومعادن أخرى ايضّا.

Mr. Hani

They're used to make jewelry, make buildings, and help our country grow.

أنها تستخدم لصناعة المجوهرات والمباني وتساعد بلدنا على النمو

Mona

Well done, class.

احسنتم أبها الفصل.

Listening and Reading
Pop Quiz

Hidden Treasures

Under the earth, we find precious mineral resources like gold, diamond, copper, and iron. These are used every day to make important things.

Gold is soft, shiny, and easy to shape. People use it for coins and jewelry. I love gold rings!

Diamond is a rare mineral - even more valuable than gold!

Copper is a strong metal which we use for electrical wires, pipes, and tools. It's cheaper than gold. Egypt gets most of its copper and gold from the Eastern Desert.

Iron is super strong. It helps make cars, ships, airplanes, and bridges. It's found in many places in Egypt.

To get minerals, miners dig deep into the ground. But minerals are non-renewable - they take millions of years to form! So we must use them wisely and never waste them.

B Comparative forms with long adjectives

With most long adjectives 2 syllables, use more or less before the adjective and than after. تتحول الصفات الطويلة إلى صيغة المقارنة كالتالي

Listening and Reading

Just Watch!-- a story by the Little Digger

رفقط راقب قصة من الحفار الصغير

ندرب على ٣١ من قطع الفهم ونصوص الاستماع

The Beginning

Everyone admired the big ship. It was huge, and proud as it sailed across the ocean. I was just a little digger on the shore -- not big, not popular -- but always ready to help.

كان الجميع يعجبون بالسفينة الكبيرة كانت ضخمة ومهيبة وهى تبحر عبر المحيط أما أنا فكنت مجرد حفار صغير على الشاطئ -لست كبيزا ولا مشهورا -لكنى دائقا مستعد للمساعدة.

One morning, I watched the big ship come close to shore to load some cargo. But then, something went wrong. It got too close and got stuck in the thick, heavy mud.

The Middle

The ship tried and tried to move, but it couldn't. Other big machines rushed to help, but none of them could pull it out.




ف صباح أحد الأيام رأيت السفينة الكبيرة تتر من الشا لتحميل بعض بضائع لكن حدث نئ خاط اقتربت كثيزا وغرقت ف الطين الكثيف الثقيل. حاولت السفينة مرازا وتكرازا أن تتحرك لكنها لم تسطيع. اسرعت آلات كبيرة أخرى للمساعدة لكن لم يتمكن أي منها من إخراجها.

Then I saw everyone starting to give up. I can help! I said. The big machines stared at me and laughed. You ?! You're so small! But I didn't let that bother me. I smiled and said, Just watch! I got to work right away, digging carefully around the ship and clearing the mud. It took a long time, but I didn't stop.

ثم رأيت الجميع يبدأون في الاستسلام فلت أنا استطيع المساعدة. حذقت الآلات الكبيرة بي وضحكت أنت أنت صغير جذا. لكنى لم اسمح لذلك أن يزعجنى ابتسمت وفلت : فقط راقب. بدأت العمل على الفور أحفر بعناية حول السفينة وأزيل الطين. استغرق الأمر وقتا طويلا لكنى لم أتوقف.

Finally, the path was clear, and the big ship floated free! The ship looked at me, amazed. Thank you, little digger! I didn't think someone so small could help me. I smiled again and said, Size doesn't matter. What matters is using your strength to help others.

وأخيزا .أصبح الطريق خاليا وعادت السفينة الكبيرة لتطفو بحرية نظرت إل السفينة بدهشة. شكزا لك أها الحفار الصغيرا لم أكن أظن أن شخضا صغيرا مثلك يمكن أن يساعدني. ابتسمت مرة اخرى وقلت الحجم لا يهم شيثا. الذى يهم شيا هو استخدام قوتك في مساعدة الاخرين.

Determine the meaning of the underlined word

- a. near
- b. peaceful
- c. happy
- d. new

وضح معاني الكلمات.

2. The opposite of the underlined word heavy is:

- a. light
- b. big
- c. huge
- d. happy

The moral of the story

المغرى من القصة:

Size doesn't matter. What matters is using strength to help others.

الحجم لا يهم ما يهم هو استخدام الفوى كي تساعد الأخرين.







Pronunciation

The prefix and stressed syllable

1 A prefix is a group of letters that is added before a word to change its meaning.

البادئة-هى مجموعة حروف تضاف قبل الكلمة لتغير معناها

| A prefix                 | dis-                | bi--                                        |
| ------------------------ | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| - mis---                 | dig-                | - means not. البادثةتعى                     |
| - dis- like - dislike    | يكره                | ex He dislikes playing football.            |
| mis -- means wrongly..   | البادئة تىبشكل خاطئ |                                             |
| -- mis- spell - misspell | يخطئ في التهجى      | ex I always misspell the word necessary     |
| -- mis place - misplace  | يضع شى في غير مكانة | e I often misplace my glasses.              |
| -- means two.            | البادنة -تعىانين    |                                             |
| - bi- cucle - bicycle    | دراجة               | ex I rode my bicycle to the park yesterday. |

2 A stressed syllable

If the base word has one syllable, the stress is on the base word

عندما تكون الكلمة ذات مقطع واحد. بكون التشديد على الكلمة الأصلية.

If the base word has two syllables, the stress is often on the prefix.

ذا كانت الكلمة تتكون من مقطعين يكون التشديد على البادئة غالبا

الراة 176

أمثلة على الكلمات الأصلية ذات مقطع واحد

| -is                                                                  | place     |
| فكلماتوالمقطع المشدد هوويس البادة                                    |           |
| mis                                                                  |           |
| -bicycle                                                             | prefix    |
| -bi                                                                  | cy-cle    |
| stressed syllable two syllables                                      |           |
| لاظفي كلمةiالمقطع المشدد هو البادثةi أن الكلمة الأصليةتكون من مقطعين |           |

Writing Skill

Writing object -- Perspective Narratives

كتابة سرد من منظور الأشياء

To write object-perspective narratives use:

لكتابة سردأون منظور الأشياء استخدم

- First Person Use I, me, my with number 2.
- ضمير المتكلم استخدم مع رقم 2

Non-living thing -- What are you? Water? Copper? Oil?

شى غير حى من أنت ٢ ماء ٢ نحاس ٢ نفط

Description

Use the 5 senses and other adjectives.

الوصف استخدم الحواس الخمسة والصفات الأخرى.

Ending

Why are you important?

النهاية لماذا أنت مهم

This type of writing where non-human things like objects, animals, or natural resources speak improves your creativity, and improves your descriptive writing skills.

هذا النوع من الكتابة حيث تتحدث الأشياء غير البشرية مثل الأشياء والحيوانات أو الموارد الطبيعية يحسن إبداعك ويحسن مهاراتك في الكتابة الوصفية.

Example:

I Am Gold

I sleep deep under the earth, hidden and quiet. People search for me, dream about me, and admire me. I sit in the crowns of kings and the rings of queens. I am soft and beautiful. I am treasured because I am valuable. I am gold.

Reading Comprehension

Demonstrate understanding of specific details

Step 1: اقرأ وتعلم كيفية الإجابة

Step 2: Read and learn how to answer:

Iron is a super strong metal. So it is perfect for making cars, ships, and airplanes. It is also used in bridges. However, iron is less expensive than gold and diamond. Iron is found in the Deserts of Egypt. To get minerals, miners dig deep into the ground. But minerals are non-renewable, so we must use them wisely and never waste them.

Water is one of the most important natural resources on our planet. We must use water wisely to save it. So it's our responsibility to save water. It's important to take short showers. We should turn off the faucet while brushing our teeth.

1. Importan topics

تعتبر مهارة الكتابة من المهارات الأساسية للتعبير عن فكرة أو موضوع لذا يجب استخدام جمل وأفكار مترابطة. قم بقراءة هذه الجمل لتساعدك على التعامل مع مهارة الكتابة (٣٣ جملة).

2. Natural resources

Natural resources are things we get from nature. The Nile River is the most important water resource in Egypt.2 It gives us water for drinking and farming.3 Oil and natural gas are also important resources.4 They are used for making fuel and electricity.5 Natural resources are very important so we must use them wisely.6

3. Minerals in our world

Gold, diamond, copper, and iron are important mineral resources.7 Each mineral has its special qualities. Gold is shiny and easy to shape.8 Diamond is more valuable than gold because it is rare.10 Copper is used for making electrical wires.11 Iron is a super strong metal.12 So it is perfect for making cars and airplanes.13

4. Water

Water is very important.14 We can see it in a glass, river, or cloud.15 It can be quiet or wild.16 It sleeps in lakes and dances in the rain.17 People drink it when they are thirsty.18 Water can shape the world.19

5. Gold

We find gold under the earth.20 People search for it and dream about it.21 It is found in the crowns of kings and the rings of queens.22 It is soft and beautiful.23

Under the earth, we find precious materials called mineral resources. These resources are very important in our daily lives. They include gold, diamond, copper, and iron. Each mineral has its special qualities. All minerals are non-renewable, so we should use them wisely. Miners work hard to get these resources from the underground.

Some minerals, like diamonds, are shiny and used in jewelry, while others, like copper, help electricity move.






Mineral Resources

Main points on the story

The Beginning

Billie Wind loved science and wanted to be a scientist - تحب Billie مادة العلوم وترغب في أن تصبح عالمة.

Billie Wind's family believed that the Earth could talk and share wisdom - تعتقد أسرة Billie إن الأرض يمكنها التحدث ونشر الحكمة.

Billie told elders she didn't believe the Earth could talk - أخبرت Billie كبار عائلتها أنها لا تصدق أن الأرض تتكلم.

Elders asked Billie to go into Everglades National Park and listen to the Earth - طلب كبار العائلة من Billie الذهاب إلي منتزه الوطني والاستماع إلى الأرض.

Billie packed her canoe and went into the Everglades - حزمت Billie أمتعتها وذهبت إلى مستنقع بقارب صغير.

The Middle

In the Everglades, Billie had no one to help her, no shelter and no food - لم يكن لدى Billie مأوى طعام أو أحد ليساعدها في منتزه.

Billie felt afraid and wished to go home - شعرت Billie بالخوف وكانت تأمل في العودة إلى منزلها.

Billie began to pay attention to the world around her slowly - بدأت Billie تنتبه للعالم من حولها ببطء.

Billie saw how animals survived - رأت Billie كيف تنجو الحيوانات.

Billie realized nature had its own way of teaching - أدركت Billie أن الطبيعة تعلم بطريقتها الخاصة.

The End

Billie learned how to listen to Earth - تعلمت Billie كيف تستمع إلى الأرض.

The Earth could talk through the sounds of the wind, the movements of animals, and the changes in land - يمكن للأرض التحدث من خلال أصوات الرياح، حركات الحيوانات، والتغيرات التي تحدث للأرض.

If people did not take care of nature, the balance would be broken - إذا لم يعتن الناس بالطبيعة سيختل التوازن على الأرض.

How the animals taught Billie to survive

She watched the animals closely and saw how they survived. The turtles hid in their shells when danger was near. The birds warned each other. Billie realized that nature had its own way of teaching. She learned how to catch fish, make a fire, and find shelter, just like the animals did.

نهاية القصة

Billie learned how to listen to the Earth.

تعلمت Billie كيف تستمع إلى الأرض

After many days in the wild, Billie Wind was no longer afraid. She learned how to survive, but more importantly, she learned how to listen. The Earth did talk, not with words, but through the sounds of the wind, the movements of animals, and the changes in the land. Everything in nature was interconnected, and if people did not take care of it, the balance would be broken.

بعد أيام كثيرة في البرية, لم تعد Billie خائفة. لقد تعلمت كيف تنجو لكن الأهم من ذلك أنها تعلمت كيف تنصت. فالأرض تتحدث ليس بالكلمات بل من خلال أصوات الرياح وحركات الحيوانات والتغيرات في الأرض. كان كل شئ في الطبيعة مترابطاً وإذا لم يعتن الناس بها فإن التوازن سيتحطم.

II  Language in use

Unit 4

Prepositions of time

We use at with clock times, mealtimes and time phrases.

ex. I get up at 6 o'clock every day

We use in with parts of day, months, and seasons.

ex. The weather is hot in summer

We use on with days of the week and time phrases.

ex. My birthday is on January 5th.

Unit 5

Comparative adjectives

للمقارنة بين شخصين أو شيئين باستخدام الصفة القصيرة نستخدم

ex. Summer is hotter than winter.

للمقارنة بين اثنين أو شيئين باستخدام الصيغة الطويلة نستخدم:

more/less + الصفة الطويلة + than

ex. Copper is less expensive than gold.

Irregular comparative forms:

- good - better
- bad - worse
- little - less
- many/much - more

Doctors, nurses and paramedics have a very important role in our life. They work around the clock. They make great efforts to protect people and their families. We should thank them by caring for our health, following their advice and respecting them. Health care workers are really our everyday heroes.

Trees are tall plants. They are very important for our Earth. Trees help clean the air. Trees give us shade, too. They give us oxygen. People and animals need oxygen to breathe. Many animals make their homes in trees. Some trees can also give us food.

The Nile River is the longest river in the world. It's important for both animals and people. Different kinds of colorful fish live in the River Nile. They eat water weeds. We use water of the Nile for drinking and growing plants. We need to keep it clean and safe.

The Nile River is the longest river in the world. The Nile is the most important water source in Egypt. It gives us water for drinking and farming. The land near the Nile is fertile. It helps us grow crops.

ية الة 228





Answers of Topics

Unit

General Exercises on Lesson

Desert animals

The desert is hot and dry, but many animals live there such as camels, snakes, and foxes. Snakes often eat small animals like rats or lizards. Camels live in the desert, too. They eat plants to stay strong. Lizards eat insects, which give them energy. Each animal has an important role in the desert.

General Exercises on Lesson

Your favorite Egyptian food

My favorite Egyptian food is koshari. Koshari is very yummy. Koshari is a tasty Egyptian meal with simple ingredients in one bowl. People pour the red sauce over it, and put the crispy onions on top.

General Exercises on Unit 1

The Nile River

There are many rivers around the world. The Nile River is one of the longest rivers in the world. It is home to many different animals. Crocodiles are strong and dangerous. They hunt large animals in or near the water. Small fish in the river eat water weeds. Birds eat insects, small fish, and plants near the river.

Unit 2

General Exercises on Lesson

Playing sports

Sports are very important. Sports help us stay healthy and make our bodies stronger. Football makes our heart strong and helps our muscles grow. Swimming is very good, too. It makes our lungs strong. Exercising regularly helps you sleep better and reduce stress.

General Exercises on Lesson

Your habits

Healthy habits are important for our bodies and minds. They help us feel good and do well. We should eat a lot of fruits and vegetables. Fruits and vegetables give us important vitamins. We should also drink a lot of water.

General Exercises on Lesson 3

Our heart

The heart is one of the most important organs of our body. It is about the size of your fist. The heart's job is to pump blood to all parts of the body. The heart is a strong muscle that keeps us alive.

General Exercises on Lesson 8

Healthcare workers

Healthcare workers like doctors and nurses are real-life heroes. They work day and night to keep people healthy and treat illnesses. Doctors use their knowledge to check patients and provide treatments. Nurses often work around the clock to give support and care for patients.

General Exercises on Unit

The job of the doctor

Doctors are very important to us. They spend their lives helping others. They work day and night to keep people healthy and treat illnesses. Doctors use their knowledge and skills to check patients, diagnose diseases, and provide treatments.







Unit 3

General Exercises on Lesson

Extreme weather

Droughts, wildfires, and heat waves are extreme weather events. Droughts happen when there is no rain for a long time. The land gets dry. Animals can find water. Wildfires are dangerous. Wildfires burn through forests and homes. Wildfires spread quickly when it's dry. Animals lose their homes because of wildfires.

General Exercises on Lesson

The weather in Ancient Egypt

Ancient Egypt had weather that made farming possible. The weather in Ancient Egypt brought some problems like floods, droughts, and sandstorms. Sandstorms brought strong hot winds with sand. It was hard to see and breathe. People stayed inside during these storms to stay safe. They built their homes with thick mud bricks to protect themselves from the wind and sand.

General Exercises on Unit 3

Floods in Ancient Egypt

The Nile River flooded every year in Ancient Egypt. The water brought rich soil to the land which helped crops grow. Sometimes the floods were too strong and damaged farms. The Ancient Egyptians built canals and basins to control the water. These canals helped to spread water over their fields.

General Exercises on Lesson

Your favorite places in your town

There are many places in our town for food, drink, or fun. We can relax and drink coffee at a café. We can buy groceries at a supermarket. We can eat dinner at a restaurant. We can go on rides at a theme park. We can buy bread at a bakery. We can go to the club to play sports.

General Exercises on Lesson

Your village

Life in a village is peaceful and simple. People live in cozy cottages surrounded by green fields and fresh air. There is a market in the main square where people buy and sell fresh fruit. Farmers keep their animals in barns. Children have fun at the community center.

General Exercises on Lessons

The New Administrative Capital

The New Administrative Capital of Egypt is a new city in Cairo. It helps reduce crowding in Cairo. It has government offices and important buildings. The city has green areas everywhere. People can find great jobs there.

General Exercises on Unit

Places in your community

In my community, there are many wonderful places. These places make our lives easier. There are green spaces, playgrounds, and a lot of parks. There is also a new library in our area. It has many books. The shopping center here is amazing. It has supermarkets, a bakery, and clothes shops. These new places help us make our community a better place to live.







General Exercises on Lesson 1

Natural resources

Natural resources are things we get from nature. The Nile River is the most important water resource in Egypt. It gives us water for drinking and farming. Oil and natural gas are also important resources. They are used for making fuel and electricity. Natural resources are very important so we must use them wisely.

General Exercises on Lesson 2

Mral

Gold, diamond, copper, and iron are important mineral resources. Each mineral has its special qualities. Gold is shiny and easy to shape. Diamond is more valuable than gold because it is rare. Copper is used for making electrical wires. Iron is a super strong metal. So it is perfect for making cars and planes.

General Exercises on Lesson 3

Helping others

Helping others is a good thing. When someone needs help, we should try to support them. It doesn't matter if we are big or small, strong or weak. What really matters is having a kind heart. Sometimes people think that only big or important people can help. But even a small person can do something great.

General Exercises on Lessons 85

Water

Water is very important. We can see it in a glass, river or cloud. It can be quiet or wild. It sleeps in lakes and dances in the rain. People drink it when they are thirsty. Water can shape the world.

General Exercises on Unit 5

Gold

Gold is soft, shiny, and easy to shape. People use it for coins and jewelry. To get gold, miners dig deep into the ground. But minerals are non-renewable, so we must use them wisely and never waste them.

PART 1

مهارة الكتابة

لا تقتصر مهارة الكتابة على استخدام القلم للكتابة في سطور فارغة ولكن مهارة الكتابة هي فن التعبير عن الأفكار. اجعل طفلك دائماً يستمتع بمهارة الكتابة ولا تجعلها ثقلاً عليه.

كيف تتعامل مع كراسة المعاصر لتنمية مهارة الكتابة مع كل وحدة على حده

1. أتبع الخطوات التالية
2. الخطوة الأولى: تهيئة الطالب للتعامل مع مهارة الكتابة باستخدام المفردات والوظائف اللغوية لكل وحدة.
3. الخطوة الثانية: التدرب على المفردات اللغوية للوحدة.
4. الخطوة الثالثة: التدرب على الوظائف اللغوية للوحدة.
5. الخطوة الرابعة: التدرب على كتابة جمل مرتبطة بالمفردات والوظائف اللغوية التي تم دراستها.

قيم نفسك

1. هل تتذكر المفردات اللغوية التي درستها في الوحدة؟
2. هل تتذكر الوظائف اللغوية التي درستها في الوحدة؟
3. هل لديك القدرة على كتابة وقراءة الكلمات والجمل الخاصة بالوحدة؟

القواعد العامة لكتابة فقرة إنشائية:

1. اترك مسافة تعادل خمسة أحرف (تقيتا 1سم في السطر الأول فقط من الموضوع)
2. أن تبدأ كل جملة بحرف
3. أن تضع  واضحة في نهاية كل جملة.
4. إتبع كل علامات الترقيم
5. إبداأ جملتك بالفاعل ثم الفعل وتوع بدايات الجمل.
6. استخدم الزمن الصحيح والمناسب للموضوع الذى تكتبه.
7. عليك باستخدام الجمل البسيطة وأن يكون لكل جملة معنى واضح.
8. اجتهد ف تحسين خطك وذلك بترك مسافة بين كل كلمة وأخرى
9. استخدم القواعد بالشكل الصحيح.

Writing a recipe using sequence words

كتابة وصفة طهى باستخدام كلمات التسلسل والتتابع:

Sequence words

When writing a recipe, organize the steps using sequence words.

عند كتابة وصفة طعام نحتاج إلى كلمات التتابع والتسلسل لترتيب الخطوات.

- First, .أولا
- Then,.ثم
- After that. ..    بعدذلك
- Next,  التالى
- Finally. اخيزا

الاة 248




Instructions for writing a recipe of your favorite food

Instructions for writing a recipe of your favorite food

تعليمات لكتابة وصفة طهى عن طعامك المفضل

1. Choose your favorite food.

اختر طعامك المفَضل.
2. Think about how it's made.

فكيعدهمبأنعللاوماذا يأعدذك

What Happens first? What comes next?
3. Use sequencing words to organize your steps.

استخدم كلمات التتابع والتسلسل لتنظيم خطواتك.
4. Write 30 -- 40 words showing the steps.

اكتب من ٤٣كلمة لتوضيح هذه الخطوات.
5. Add a title : How to make Your food name

اضف عنوان كيف تصنع اسم الطعام
6. Glue or draw a picture of your favorite food.

.الصق او ارسم صورة لطعامك المفضل.

Creating a poster

تصميم ملصق

The elements of a good poster

-عناصر الملصق الجيد:

- A clear title to grab the attention. -عنوانا واضخا لجذب الانتباه
- Organized steps to explain. -خطوات منظمة للتوضيح.
- Photos or drawings to illustrate. -الصور ورسومات للتوضيح.

Writing a fiction story

كتابة قصة خيالية :

Fiction story is a made-up story created from the imagination, not based on real things that happened.

القصة الخيالية :هى قصة من وحى الخيال لم تحدث في الحقيقة أى أنها ليست قصة واقعية.

The plot of the fiction story has

عناصر القصة الخيالية هى

- Beginning. Meet the characters and setting - البداية :التعرف على شخصيات القصة ومكان وزمان القصة
- Problem. Something goes wrong. - المشكلة :أى حدوث خطأً ما
- Plot Middle ... the character tries to fix it. - الحبكة المنتصف: يقوم الأشخاص بمحاولة إصلاح هذا الخطأ
- Climax ... The most exciting part - الذروة :الجزء الأكثر تشويقا في القصة
- Ending ... The problem is solved - نهاية القصة: يتم فيها حل المشكلة






Writing a Narrative Text

كتابة نص سردى :

Writing a Narrative Text in the First Person:

كتابة نص سردى بصيفة المتكلم

1. First Person: Use I, me, my.

الشخص الأولاستخدم mعندما تكتب فصة بصيفة المتكلم كأنك تحكى قصة
2. Setting: Where is your story happening?

عن نفسك.
3. Goal Setting: What do you want to do?

تحديد الهدف:ماذا تريد أن تفعلوضع ما هو هدفك ف القصة
4. Description: Use the 5 senses and other adjectives.

الوصف:استخدم الحواس الخمس الصفات الأخرىماذارأيتسمعت مع الصفات لتوضيح
5. Ending: How does the story finish?

المشهد مثل كبير. النهاية : :كيف تنتهى القصة ٢ يجب أن تضع نهاية واضحة للقصة

Notes

The word narrative comes from narration or telling a story.

الكلمة arativتعنىسرد أو رواية قصة

Don't forget that a good story is one that readers can see in their minds.

ا تنن فص لدة هتلكل تجعلالاخل الأحدافي عقل

Good writers use a lot of description to do this.

الكتاب الجيدون يستخدمون الكثير من الوصف لجعل الفصة حيّةومشوفة

Pop Quiz on Vocabulary

Choose the correct answer from a, b, c or d.

1. ______ are huge and scary animals.
- a. Turtles
- b. Crocodiles
- c. Weeds
- d. Lizards
2. To cook a meal correctly, you should follow a ______.
- a. map
- b. flyer
- c. recipe
- d. book
3. On hot, sunny days it is nice to sit in the ______ of a tree.
- a. trunk
- b. branch
- c. shade
- d. root
4. A person who does not like to share their toys is ______.
- a. happy
- b. kind
- c. selfish
- d. friendly
5. Lions ______ other animals to get their food.
- a. play
- b. hunt
- c. sleep
- d. run
6. The main, thick part of a tree is called its ______.
- a. leaf
- b. trunk
- c. flower
- d. shade

Unit 1 The Present Simple Tense

2 Adverbs of frequency

ظروف التكرار :

- always دانفا

1. The Story

Main points of the story

1. Billie Wind was a young Native American girl who lived in Florida.

يلفتاة أمريكية الأصل تعيش ف ولاية فلوريدا.
2. Billie Wind loved science and dreamed of becoming a scientist.

تحب Bili Wi مادة العلوم وتحلم أن تصبح عالمة.
3. Billie Wind's family believed the Earth could talk and share wisdom.

تعتقد أسرة ilie i أن الأرض تستطيع التحدث ونقل الحكمة.
4. Billie told the elders she didn't believe the Earth could talk.

أخبرت كبار عائلتها أنها لا تصدق أن الأرض تتكلم.
5. The elders asked Billie to go into Everglades National Park and listen to the Earth.

طلب كبار العائلة من الذهاب إلى محمية الوطنية وتستمع إلى الأرض.
6. In the Everglades, Billie had no one to help her, no shelter, and no food.

لم يكن لديها مأوى وطعام وأحد يساعدها في محمية.
7. At night, Billie heard alligators and owls.

في الليل كانت تسمع أصوات التماسيح والبوم.
8. Billie wished she was back home, safe and warm.

كانت تأمل في العودة إلى منزلها في أمان ودفء.
9. Billie began to pay attention to the world around her slowly.

بدأت i تولي اهتماما للعالم من حولها.
10. Billie saw how animals survived. The alligators waited for food and turtles hid from danger in their shells.

أت i كيف تعيش الحيوانات فالتماسيح تنظر طعامها السلاحف تختبئ عن الخطر داخل أصدافها.
11. Billie realized that nature had its own way of teaching.

أدركت illie أن للطبيعة طريقتها الخاصة لتعلمنا.
12. Billie learned how to listen to Earth.

تعلمت Bilie كيف تستمع إلى الأرض.
13. The Earth could talk through the sounds of the wind, the movements of animals, and the changes in land.

فالأرض يمكنها التحدث من خلال أصوات الرياح، حركات الحيوانات، والتغييرات التي تحدث للأرض.

Billie Wind was sent into the swamp alone. At first, Billie struggled to survive in the Everglades. She had no shelter, no food, and no one to help her. The swamp was full of strange sounds, and she felt afraid. At night, she heard alligators splashing in the water and owls hooting in the trees. She wished she was back home, safe and warm.

- -believe-
- -wisdom-
- -scientist-
- -animals-
- -vet-

Billie Wind was a young Native American girl who lived in Florida. She loved science and dreamed of becoming a scientist when she grew up. Her family believed that the Earth could talk and share wisdom with those who listened to it. They said the wind, the water, and the animals all had something to teach. Billie did not believe this. She thought these were just old stories with no real meaning.




34 2. Writing Skill

Important Topics

How to stay healthy

Having healthy habits is very important for a strong body. We should eat healthy food like fruits and vegetables. They give us important vitamins. We should exercise regularly. Exercising helps us sleep better and reduces stress. It also helps us lose weight and makes our bones stronger.

The Nile River

The Nile River is very important to Egypt. It gives us freshwater for drinking and farming. The land near the Nile is very fertile, so farmers can grow crops like cotton and wheat. The river is also a home for many animals, like fish, birds, and crocodiles. We must keep the Nile clean.

Places in my community

There are many important places in my community. The supermarket is where we buy groceries. The bakery sells fresh bread and cakes. I go to the club with my friends to play sports and have fun. At the coffee shop, people can relax and meet their friends. All these places help make our community a great place to live in.

Extreme weather

Extreme weather can be dangerous. A flood happens when there is too much rain, and water covers the streets. A drought is a long time without rain, which makes the land very dry. A wildfire is a big fire that burns forests and homes. It is important to know about extreme weather so we can stay safe.

Natural resources in Egypt

Egypt is a country rich in natural resources. The Nile River is our most important water resource. We also have fertile soil for farming. We have oil and natural gas, which give us electricity. Egypt's deserts contain gold, iron, and other minerals. We must use them wisely.

The New Administrative Capital

The New Administrative Capital is a modern city in Egypt. It helps reduce crowding in Cairo. The city is







Modern Technology

Modern technology is used to manage roads and energy. It has government offices and important buildings. It has the tallest building in Africa, the Iconic Tower. People can find great jobs and have a peaceful life there.

Sports

It is important to play sports. Sports make our bodies stronger. Football helps our muscles grow. It also increases energy. Swimming makes our lungs strong. It helps our whole body. Playing sports regularly is also good for our mental health.

Healthcare Heroes

Healthcare workers are our everyday heroes. Doctors diagnose diseases and help us get better. Nurses work around the clock to give support and care for patients. Paramedics respond quickly in emergencies and take patients to the hospital safely. We should thank these heroes by caring for our health and showing them respect.

The desert is a hot and dry place, but many amazing animals live there. Snakes and lizards are common. Snakes often hunt rats, while lizards eat insects to get energy. Camels are famous desert animals. They are special because they can survive for a long time without water. They eat plants to stay strong. Many desert animals, like owls, come out at night when the weather is cooler. Every animal has an important role to help nature stay in balance.

There are many rivers around the world. The Nile River is one of the longest rivers in the world. It is home to many different animals. Crocodiles are strong and dangerous. They hunt large animals in or near the water. Small fish in the river eat water weeds. Birds eat insects, small fish, and plants near the river.

Healthcare workers are our real-life heroes. Doctors use their skills to diagnose diseases and provide treatment. Nurses work around the clock to give support and care for patients. Paramedics are also very important. They respond quickly in emergencies and give help on the spot. They take patients safely to hospitals.

All these people work together to help us live healthier lives. We should always thank them by taking care of our own health and showing them respect for their hard work.

Extreme weather is very different from the usual weather we see every day. It can be much stronger and more dangerous. Examples of extreme weather include heavy storms, hot heat waves, strong sandstorms, big floods, and long droughts. These weather events can damage houses, roads, and farms. People and animals may suffer when extreme weather happens. That's why it's important to learn about these events and how to stay safe during them. They can happen anywhere in the world.
