Read and complete the text with the words in the box.

- Selfish strong apples - weak

Tom had a healthy apple tree. The tree gave Tom apples and Tom played on it. Tom traveled away and when he came back, the tree was weak. Tom knew that he was selfish. So he decided to take care of the tree. The tree grew strong again.

Read the text and answer the questions.

Under the earth, we find precious mineral resources like gold, diamond, copper, and iron. These are used every day to make important things.

Gold is soft, shiny, and easy to shape. People use it for coins and jewelry. I love gold rings! Diamond is a rare mineral - even more valuable than gold!

Copper is a strong metal which we use for electrical wires, pipes, and tools. It's cheaper than gold. Egypt gets most of its copper and gold from the Eastern Desert. Iron is super strong. It helps make cars, ships, airplanes, and bridges. It's found in many places in Egypt.

To get minerals, miners dig deep into the ground. But minerals are non-renewable - they take millions of years to form! So we must use them wisely and never waste them.

Ancient Egypt had weather that made farming possible, even in the desert. The Nile River gave water and rich soil, which helped crops grow. But sometimes, the weather also brought problems like floods, droughts, and sandstorms.

Guiding elements: Healthcare heroes, paramedics, patients

295




Listening Texts

نصوص الاستماع

Sample test

My uncle lives in a small village. He lives in a cozy cottage with a big garden. On Saturdays, he goes to the main square to sell vegetables from his farm. He keeps his cows and chickens in a big barn. At night, my uncle walks near the fields and looks at the stars.

Sample test 2

Last summer, there was a terrible heat wave. It was very hot for many days. The land became very dry, and some small ponds dried up completely. We had to stay inside during the day to be safe. We drank lots of water to stay cool and healthy.

Sample test 3

I'm Rang: My favorite food is koshari. It's a famous Egyptian meal. To make it, we first cook rice and pasta. Then, we boil some lentils. The red sauce is made with tomatoes and garlic. Finally, we put it all in a bowl and add crispy onions on top.

Sample test

I'm Mazen. My favorite place in my neighborhood is the coffee shop. I go there with my dad on Fridays. I usually have a piece of chocolate cake and my dad drinks coffee. It's a relaxing place. The baker at the shop next door makes the best bread.

Sample test

My friend Ali is a very healthy boy. He never eats unhealthy food. He always eats fruits and vegetables. He usually plays sports after school at the club. He is very active and fit. Ali exercises regularly because this helps him feel strong and happy.

Sample test

I'm Marwan. My family and I live in the New Administrative Capital. It is a new city, 45 km east of Cairo. It was built to reduce crowding. The city is very smart and uses modern technology. It has the tallest building in Africa, the Iconic Tower.

Sample test

The heart is a strong muscle that keeps us alive. Too much stress can hurt your heart. It is important to keep your heart healthy. You can take care of your heart by eating good food and sleeping well. Exercise helps your heart pump better and keeps you from getting sick.

Sample test

I'm Dalia. Last week, the weather was bad in my city. A lot of rain caused a flood. The water covered the streets and some cars got stuck. My uncle's house was damaged. The water flooded his living room, and he had to stay with us for a few days.

Sample test

My friend Tamer and Ali went to the theme park last Friday. It was very exciting. We went on many rides. We were very happy and excited. In the evening we were hungry, so we ate some pizza at a restaurant in the park. It was a fun day.

Sample test 10

Gold is a precious mineral found under the earth. It is a soft, shiny metal. People use it to make beautiful jewelry like rings and necklaces. Because it is rare and beautiful, it is very valuable. Miners have to dig deep into the ground to find it.
